# Walk the Plank: the captain's question bank.
# One prompt per line:  prompt text | answer, answer=alias=alias, ...
# Answers are listed roughly from most common to least; the first three carry the heaviest prior.
# Lines starting with # are ignored. Everything is matched case-insensitively with light fuzzing.
